<a id="p13b2a-shared-pending-journal-and-recovery-boundary-2026-10-21"></a>
## P13b2a shared pending journal and recovery boundary — 2026-10-21

- **Design and scope:** P13b2 recovery contract #530 was accepted and merged at pinned base `e56c8c32ee1d74c3f63838e15909200adbbe4dfa`. This implementation batch delivers a project/app/version-namespaced canonical journal limited to `{commandId,datasetId,uid}`, strict raw JSON/key-value validation, a hard 1,000-record cap with no eviction, complete scans and UI pages of at most 100, browser Web Locks for cross-tab read/write/conditional cleanup/migration, and an explicit memory adapter only for tests/Node fixtures. The central `commitCommand` path writes and reads back the identity after server preflight and before starting the Firestore transaction; transaction callbacks contain no journal I/O. Receipt confirmation fences server UID/dataset/lease context before and after lookup, and only an exact matching receipt can remove its exact journal row. Missing/read-error/in-flight/different-dataset rows remain visible and block the appropriate actions; a confirmed server-null item requires explicit manual continuation and remains persistent. Initial AuthGate recovery completes before Cloud sections mount, and §7.4 close restrictions remain active. The legacy order slot migrates only after canonical readback; the unsafe “confirm not posted and clear” control is removed.
- **TDD lineage and tests:** RED `d1b0c54e8acc569c40318ca891a35fae982d2ba3` is a separate failing behavioral test commit; GREEN `f2decd9e07dcf41b6eb43f181b3eedd35a96fbbc` is a separate implementation commit. The actual browser/Emulator P13b2a suite passed 5/5 (`/tmp/easyorder-p13b2a-p13b2-focused-final.log`): legacy migration/manual continuation, 101-row bounded pages, a real Emulator commit confirmed after reload without a second effect even when cleanup was denied, mounted-shell behavior under another-tab changes and the shared exclusive Web Lock, plus real two-page concurrent writes, UUID collision rejection and the last available capacity slot. The test inspects persisted keys and raw JSON shape; only the three allowlisted identity fields appear.
- **Local verification at the P13b2a implementation GREEN head:** full Vitest passed 182 files / 1,988 tests; 16 Emulator-only files / 747 tests skipped without Emulator config (`/tmp/easyorder-p13b2a-full-vitest.log`). Required command/Rules/repository/journal Emulator gate passed 3 files / 209 tests with `demo-easyorder` (`/tmp/easyorder-p13b2a-command-emulator-final2.log`; machine-readable report `/tmp/p13b2a-command-proof-final2.json`). P14 cloud backup Emulator regression passed 24/24 (`/tmp/easyorder-p13b2a-backup-emulator-final.log`). Full shared `npm run test:e2e` passed 49/49 with synthetic identities: default 24, P12c 2, prototype 7, P13a production 5, P13a StrictMode dev 1, P13a missing-config production 1, P13b1 4, P13b2a 5 (`/tmp/easyorder-p13b2a-shared-e2e-final.log`). `npm run lint`, `npm run build`, and root `npx tsc --noEmit` exited 0 (`/tmp/easyorder-p13b2a-final-lint.log`, `/tmp/easyorder-p13b2a-final-build.log`, `/tmp/easyorder-p13b2a-final-root-tsc.log`). Vite retains the existing native-config `__dirname` and >500 kB bundle warnings. Post-mitigation local results are listed in the following P05 incident entry.
- **App TypeScript baseline:** pinned base `e56c8c32ee1d74c3f63838e15909200adbbe4dfa` and candidate both exit 2 with 208 diagnostics. Normalizing only source line/column locations yields byte-identical diagnostics (`/tmp/easyorder-p13b2a-app-tsc-exact/{base,candidate}.{raw,normalized}`, `diff`, `exit`). This is inherited diagnostic parity, not a clean App TSC pass.
- **Harness corrections and limits:** the first shared E2E run revealed that the P12c Node-side repository fixture needed explicit injection of the new test-only memory journal; that fixture now injects the adapter and the final shared chain passes. P13b2a capacity browser setup initially retained its earlier UUID-collision fixture while seeding 999 rows; it now clears that fixture before the capacity-edge race, and the final real two-page test passes. P13b1 F3 regression tests now await the new startup boundary's navigation readiness and scope the “no success status” assertion to the diagnostics region; focused P13b1 passed 4/4 (`/tmp/easyorder-p13b2a-p13b1-focused-final2.log`). One command Emulator invocation ended with `clearFirestore()` returning emulator HTTP 500 before `EnsureDay`; no assertion failed in that file, and one fresh run of the exact required command passed 209/209. This transient invocation is retained in its raw log (`/tmp/easyorder-p13b2a-command-emulator-final.log`).
- **P05 provider setup incident and bounded mitigation:** source CI run [39555890249](https://github.com/cheerc/talented-easyorder/actions/runs/39555890249) at pre-mitigation head `6694a84b648a3d47f9087c4040511809b6013bbf` failed on attempts 1 and 2 in only `cloudCommands.spec.ts` P05.1 `beforeEach` `clearFirestore()`, with Emulator HTTP 500／UNKNOWN before the case body; each report was 208/209 and the other 179 command cases plus the other two files passed. The service-side cause is unknown; the local initial full P05 command also had one setup failure before a fresh exact rerun passed, and a later isolated local run passed 180/180. Coordinator ruling authorized a helper limited to the suite's initial reset and that exact structured error, a bounded single retry, no retry after first success or for other errors, and immediate preservation/upload of demo Emulator debug log plus Vitest JSON. Separate RED `16f5ede` records the missing-retry behavior (two focused cases failed, six passed); GREEN `01a89d7` implements the helper, case-boundary diagnostics and CI artifact capture. Post-fix focused setup tests passed 8/8; exact three-file demo Emulator proof exited 0 at 217/217 with zero failures/skips (`/tmp/p13b2a-p05-green-final.log`, `/tmp/p13b2a-p05-green-final.json`); full Vitest passed 183 files / 1,996 tests, 747 Emulator-only skips (`/tmp/p13b2a-full-vitest-green.log`); `npm run lint` and root `npx tsc --noEmit` exited 0. This mitigation bounds a test setup retry; it does not establish the cause of the hosted Emulator error or claim the service issue is fixed. ### R1 rework — async owner fencing and reconnect recovery — 2026-10-21

- **Review findings addressed:** every async receipt/context result is rechecked against the currently verified Auth owner; lookup and cleanup are fenced by mount, owner, lease epoch, dataset, and generation. Conditional journal removal rechecks the guard after acquiring the Web Lock and immediately before removing the exact identity. A known business success remains success if local cleanup is skipped, and the exact in-flight registration is released in its outer `finally`. Offline invalidates prior work, clears the manual acknowledgement, and gates actions; online coalesces bursts and starts a fresh complete journal/server scan. Old matched results cannot update UI or clear the journal. Recovery errors stay gated, and a previously null receipt requires a new manual acknowledgement after reconnection.
- **TDD lineage:** separate RED `fd16cb9002ec639e7c1ed2c1a8474911d674dd91` and GREEN `1c87116a433f9ba3dc85ed95f8d2554f162aa958` commits. After GREEN, the journal/recovery focused suites passed 26/26 (`npx vitest run src/firebase/__tests__/cloudPendingJournal.spec.ts src/components/cloud/reviewerRecovery.test.tsx --maxWorkers=1`); changed-file ESLint passed.

### R2 rework — synchronous journal cleanup and first-commit scope gate — 2026-10-21

- **Lineage and scope:** PR #531 remains based on `e56c8c32ee1d74c3f63838e15909200adbbe4dfa`; the R2-reviewed head was `2a74941515bc483d6bfd6b1cb0b764a8c12f4c5d`. Separate regression-only RED commit `609ed08d75a9cace915b2588ab97c14b1da15cbc` and product/test GREEN commit `c1c9541d27ff00fdf8aa011db955c4b6606d2343` preserve the required TDD lineage. No Rules, schema, route protocol or business command behavior changed.
- **R3 addressed:** receipt and server owner/dataset/lease checks remain outside the Web Lock. `removeIf` now accepts only a synchronous boolean predicate; under the lock it checks literal `true`, scans and matches the exact identity, checks the predicate again, then performs the synchronous removal without yielding. A thenable or non-`true` runtime result fails closed. All production `removeIf` callers pass local Auth/mount/generation/online predicates; repository search found no server context read in a lock callback. If a local invalidation is observed while waiting, the stale callback cannot remove the record. A confirmed business success remains successful if local cleanup is canceled or fails.
- **R4 addressed:** recovery state carries the Firestore app/database, UID, lease epoch and confirmed dataset scope. A changed db/app, UID or epoch synchronously invalidates the prior generation during render; the same render hides prior-scope recovery controls and keeps the child subtree inert until fresh matching recovery completes. Same-scope manual continuation and the R1 Auth/unmount/offline/reconnect fencing remain covered.
- **RED evidence:** before GREEN, focused probes had six expected failures across pending async guard, final-guard microtask interleaving, first changed-scope commit and local guard timing. The real Chromium/two-page Web Locks probe observed both peer write/read incomplete, one exclusive lock held and one peer lock pending while the simulated remote guard promise remained unresolved. The old behavior therefore held the namespace lock across the pending guard. The saved raw browser RED/GREEN logs are `/tmp/p531-r2-r3-red-browser.log` and `/tmp/p531-r2-r3-green-browser.log`.
- **Focused local verification:** the journal/repository/boundary/recovery command (`npx vitest run src/firebase/__tests__/cloudPendingJournal.spec.ts src/firebase/__tests__/cloudCommandRepository.failure.spec.ts src/components/cloud/__tests__/CloudPendingRecoveryBoundary.scope.test.tsx src/components/cloud/reviewerRecovery.test.tsx --maxWorkers=2`) completed with 33 passed; one Emulator-only repository file (24 tests) was skipped in that non-Emulator invocation. ### P13b2b2a Students same-mount recovery 2026-10-22

#### Initial candidate evidence (pre-r1 rework)

- **Scope and base:** branch `codex/p13b2b2a-students-recovery`, pinned base `49f224597dd9267334ff4c6e64bed4d4777f7e1a` (the merged #533 main SHA). This batch implements same-mount recovery only for the six typed Student commands: CreateStudent, EditStudent, ChangeStudentCode, SetStudentStatus, EnsureDay and CorrectOpening. It reuses the explicitly paired same-FirebaseApp Firestore Lite reader only for read-only receipt/context confirmation. Ordinary list/read operations and writes remain on the full SDK. No Rules, index, schema, Firestore initialization or production endpoint policy changed.
- **TDD lineage:** RED commit `7191e064b52326705e550b4bdc31331e2f0174a8` is separate from GREEN commit `5d9251808fb752f318dcf0f486853da316c17dd4`. Before GREEN, 18 focused component/repository assertions passed and 2 behavioral assertions failed for missing semantic confirmation and the 10-second in-flight query boundary. GREEN adds a central read-only semantic confirmation/cleanup seam, uses one canonical builder for Student writes and recovery confirmation, and retains exact frozen intent/ID/revisions/epoch across retry.
- **Behavior and safety:** the page starts its 10,000 ms warning from the actual submit and never auto-retries. A Lite not-found or error while the original promise is still in flight cannot authorize a second commit. After the original unknown/offline promise settles, a fresh fenced not-found and explicit operator action can retry only the same frozen intent and ID. A matched receipt keeps the page and navigation locked until a complete full-SDK server list is loaded. UID, db/app, identity and lease-epoch changes synchronously invalidate the old render scope; late command/receipt/list completions after scope loss or unmount cannot restore sensitive state or clear the journal. EnsureDay confirmation returns the user to an explicit CorrectOpening action and does not submit it automatically.
- **Full local unit verification:** `cd frontend && npx vitest run --maxWorkers=1` exited 0: 188 test files passed / 15 Emulator-only files skipped; 2,078 tests passed / 753 skipped. The full run includes component deferred-promise/fake-timer boundaries, semantic receipt mismatch/refusal, all six write/confirm canonical mappings, UID/epoch/db invalidation, unmount late-result fencing and opening-day non-autocorrection.

Shared Auth/Firestore Emulator Chromium chain exited 0 with 57/57: default 24, P12c 2, prototype 7, P13a production 5, P13a StrictMode 1, P13a missing-config 1, P13b1 4, P13b2 13. In P13b2, actual Student create and edit commits were held after server commit beyond 10 seconds; Lite confirmed their real receipts while the original response was held, UI/navigation stayed locked until response release and fresh server list, and one Student receipt/sequence effect remained. ### P13b2b2c DayClose same-mount recovery 2026-10-22

- **Scope and lineage:** branch `claude/p13b2b2c-day-close-recovery-vkwu9u`, based on provider main `09fa9301dbb47c5accd5361d91230e6dffd16db3` (no drift from the dispatch base). #535 facts: source head `a98e69dd7e160cbe96c46b5d25d46d20d23e75a9`, r2 review 5617465094 VERIFIED, source CI 39809757469 success, admin squash merge `09fa930`. Operator removed post-merge CI monitoring; no main CI success is claimed. RED `d06364fa676ee2d707862c818ea2c80549016306`; GREEN (repository + page) and the shared-runner browser cases are separate later commits. The batch covers the six DayClose commands: close-day, record-post-close-entry, reopen-day, backfill-opening, ensure-day and start-backfill. No Rules, schema, global writer, SDK retry or central recovery API change.
- **Recovery behavior:** `cloudDayCloseRepository` takes an explicit `recoveryReader` option; one canonical builder per intent is shared by the writers and the read-only receipt check, which never commits and never runs the alignment/previous-day preflight. `closeDay` keeps its receipt-aware nearest-next-opened-day alignment preflight and re-checks the signed-in UID and the page guard after it, before the commit. A composed, dataset-bearing `loadSnapshot` reads the head before and after the overview, selected day and lookups and requires the same dataset and seq; lookup errors disable only the affected form, authority errors invalidate the snapshot. The page keeps one operation per mounted owner (single flight, first-render mask, cleared on unmount or owner change); 9,999/10,000 ms is a status prompt only (no abort, no new ID, no automatic retry); only a fresh successful null query that began after the same attempt settled enables an explicit same-ID retry. A confirmed receipt stays locked until a snapshot of the same owner, dataset and day is accepted; a different dataset or day stays blocked, including manual reload. Cleanup authority loss revokes immediately and skips snapshot/callback; storage-only failure keeps the confirmed warning. `onLedgerChanged` fires once after the accepted snapshot.
- **§7.4 and backfill:** a new CloseDay checks the shared unresolved flag at submit and again inside the repository guard; a same-ID retry is treated as resolving its own operation and only other unresolved journal identities (or an unreadable journal) block it. The flag is never reset. A backfill plan is bound to owner, dataset, day and one explicitly initiated generation; each needed step gets its own immutable command ID, the next step is derived from fresh same-dataset/day server state, and automatic advance happens only when the previous step's own submission returned the receipt (a local-journal cleanup that finds the entry already removed is not a reason to stop; the receipt is what counts). A receipt found by a query, an unknown/rejected step, a failed refresh or an owner change stops automatic progress and needs the explicit continue. The post-close stale-reference preflight re-checks owner, user and page read generation after each await.
- **Local verification (candidate):** `CloudDayClosePage.test.tsx` 71/71; repository recovery + snapshot suites 33/33 (the related cloud component/firebase unit run passed 326/326). Full `npx vitest run` exited 0: 191 files passed, 15 Emulator-only files skipped; 2,169 passed / 753 skipped / 0 failed. P11 CloseDay Emulator proof (`EASYORDER_REQUIRE_CLOUD_DAY_CLOSE=true`, `cloudDayClose.spec.ts`) passed 198/198. `npm run lint`, `npm run build` and frontend root `npx tsc --noEmit` exited 0; Vite kept its existing `__dirname` and chunk warnings. App TSC (`tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.app.json --pretty false`) exited 2 with 208 diagnostics / 460 lines on both the pinned base and the candidate; raw output was byte-identical (inherited parity, not a clean pass). Central command/journal proofs were not rerun because no central API changed; cloud-backup proof was not run (BackupScreen untouched).
- **Browser evidence:** shared Auth/Firestore Emulator Chromium command `npx firebase emulators:exec --project demo-easyorder --config ../firebase.json --only auth,firestore "npx playwright test --config playwright.p13b2.config.ts --reporter=line"` exited 0 with 24/24 (19 existing + 5 new): CloseDay response held beyond 10 s with Lite confirmation before release (one settlement, one command, journal keeps exactly commandId/datasetId/uid), confirmed receipt locked on dataset A→B including manual reload, same-UID epoch change during cleanup revoking before the held response is released, real `Storage.prototype.removeItem` failure keeping the confirmed warning with one write, and a multi-step backfill whose held EnsureDay response never starts the next step (receipt query and response release both leave it stopped) until the explicit continue, then three distinct command IDs each exactly once. The first runs hit environment/fixture issues, not product defects: a missing Playwright browser revision (a local compatibility symlink to the installed headless shell was added outside the repo), a role-name substring match between the close and post-close forms, and lease epoch carry-over after a failed case (the cleanup helper now vacates the lease). No timeout, retry or skip was widened. Failing-before browser evidence on the RED tree was not collected: the browser cases were added with the GREEN work. All fixtures are synthetic `demo-easyorder` data.

No assertion was weakened.

The PR body is the authority for the current head, source CI attempt/jobs and counts.
#### r1 rework — confirmed-refresh failure stops automatic backfill progress

- **Disposition:** r1 review [5618475662](https://github.com/cheerc/talented-easyorder/pull/536#pullrequestreview-5618475662) rejected head `b0cde8b8c64b4378c8891ee1f3a8d276828414ea` (1 Blocking, 1 Nit); the Coordinator ruled F1 valid. F1: when the original EnsureDay submission returned its receipt but the confirmed full read then failed (OFFLINE), a later successful manual reload recomputed `auto = confirmedByResult && attemptSettled` and one read-only click submitted BackfillOpeningCash and StartBackfill. Fix: any failed page read while a plan exists marks that plan generation as never auto-advancing again; a manual reload only reads, and every further step needs the explicit continue. F2: a new component test shows a changed Firestore instance (same user and lease) masks the first render and drops the old operation without cleanup, confirmation query or ledger callback.

### P13b2b3a journal cleanup post-condition and backup recovery 2026-10-23

- **Scope and lineage:** branch `claude/p13b2b3a-backup-recovery`, based on main `90cd193` (#536 merged). Plan: `claude/plan-p13b2b3` (not merged), batch P13b2b3a with Gate 1 rulings R1/R4/R8 approved. RED `2258f132c38ebb453f93ac625fdce109b8c08ae5` (tests only, failing for the missing behavior); GREEN `2248413a14d05b5e67a63518d9fb77cfedf48ef6`; adapter unit test `f1e52d3c73150b707ca92b8801b32f89794757e6`.
- **#536 nit:** `CloudPendingJournal.removeIfPresent` reports `removed`/`absent`/`kept` under the same lock, predicate and exact-identity checks; `removeIf` is a thin wrapper that still returns `true` only on an actual removal. `cleanupConfirmedIntent` now returns `outcome !== 'kept'`, so a receipt confirmed after the commit path already cleared the journal is a clean success, while an owner/mount/lease loss that keeps the entry and a real removal failure still warn. A different identity on the same key still throws. Two existing `recoveryReader` unit tests changed only their journal stand-in (`removeIf` to `removeIfPresent`); no assertion was relaxed.
- **BackupScreen recovery (R3/R4/R8):** adapter `cloudBackupCommandRepository.ts` (a separate small file because `cloudBackupRepository.ts` is the read-only export path); `datasetId` is the exported head's `manifest.datasetId` and `uid` is the current access profile. The planned `getPendingRecoveryContext` was not exposed because nothing uses it. Without a registered Lite pair nothing is submitted (fail closed). Same mount only: a confirmed receipt shows `verifiedLocal` because the file was verified in this mount before the command was frozen; after a remount the shell gate decides and the screen claims nothing.

#### r1 rework — single flight during file verification and late authorization loss

- **Disposition:** r1 review rejected head `5171a2a1879297857780beb485f1b4b9cf845a09`; the Coordinator ruled both findings valid. Blocking: `verifySelected` checked `locked` only at entry and the backup reported no activity until `runAttempt`, so during the `verifyBackupFile` await the day-close commands stayed enabled and a stale closure could still submit RecordBackup (two commands in flight). Nit: the `runAttempt` catch path revoked on a late authorization-loss error even after a query had confirmed the receipt.
- **Fix:** the screen reports active synchronously when file verification starts (no command yet) and releases it when verification fails or is abandoned; after the await it re-checks a live `lockedRef` and submits nothing if the host has locked it; the catch path returns once a receipt is confirmed, like `finishResult`.
- **RED/GREEN:** RED `bf8a736970806e3656c9ba6a7fe7f4b240634e93` (BackupScreen: reports active from verification start, no submit when locked during verification, confirmed receipt survives a late LEASE_LOST; new `CloudDayClosePage.backupLock.test.tsx` mounts the real `CloudDayClosePage` with the real `BackupScreen` and locks commands and navigation from verification start); GREEN `e248de90c1621a0afb5cfaf96297842fd705dc81`.
- **Local verification:** full `npx vitest run` exited 0: 193 files passed / 15 skipped, 2,196 passed / 753 skipped / 0 failed; lint exit 0; `npx tsc -b` still 211 diagnostics, identical to base; shared P13b2 Chromium 26/27 with the same single local-only failure as on unmodified main. Exact-head CI is recorded in the PR body.

### P13b2b3b report export and print same-page recovery 2026-10-23

- **Scope and lineage:** branch `claude/p13b2b3b-report-recovery`, based on main `1af1209b73b516b408288107ff6399d31fd336c3` (#537 merged). Plan `claude/plan-p13b2b3` (not merged), batch P13b2b3b under the Gate 1 rulings R4 (default: the same mount that still holds the `FrozenAction` outputs the frozen content once after a query-confirmed receipt; a `RecoveryIdentity` after a remount only confirms) and R8 (separate read-only query and same-ID retry). RED `a5c19e951a8bfa66f7aa105a3a51c5e8037ca27b` (tests only: Vitest and Chromium failing for the missing behavior); GREEN `35632a4f7087d2fda6a43634b88ad3609f67eab5`.
- **Repository:** `createCloudReportActionRepository(db, { recoveryReader })` derives the command once (`toCommandInput`) for `recordExport`/`recordPrint`, `confirmPendingIntent` and `cleanupConfirmedIntent`, and exposes `getPendingRecoveryContext`; `readCommand` stays for the Emulator specs but the page no longer uses the full-SDK read for recovery.
- **Page:** one `ActionOperation` per mounted owner (attempt, attemptPromise, attemptSettled, querySequence, checking, retryAuthorized, receipt, timer). Ten seconds only prompt「結果確認中，勿重新收款」with the original command ID; the query is allowed while the original is in flight; retry needs a settled original attempt, a fresh successful Lite not-found that started after it settled, the same owner/dataset/epoch and being online (read failure, maintenance, quota, unsupported schema and different dataset never unlock it); a not-found query no longer resends by itself. A confirmed receipt (from the original result or from a query) runs `cleanupConfirmedIntent`, then outputs the frozen content once (`performedActions` still dedupes); a late success or failure of the original never outputs again nor overrides the confirmed result. `RecoveryIdentity` now carries `datasetId` and `uid` and only confirms. The dead `rebuildFrozenAction` branch was deleted, so an old report H is never rebuilt. Without an Auth uid the page clears itself and sends nothing.

(2) Recovery queries now go through `confirmPendingIntent` (Lite) instead of `readCommand`, so mock results are `matched`/`not-found`/`different-dataset`. ### P13b2b3c account history correction same-page recovery 2026-10-23

- **Scope and lineage:** branch `claude/p13b2b3c-history-recovery`, based on main `a33278ccf08425a662a7a193f1e76f2bbb5919e5` (#538 merged). Plan `claude/plan-p13b2b3` (not merged), batch P13b2b3c under Gate 1 rulings R4 (a `RecoveryIdentity` after a remount only confirms; nothing is resent) and R8 (separate read-only query and same-ID retry). RED `2c59f6243728788be10841ca58942a45d687a580` (tests only: Vitest and Chromium failing for the missing behavior); GREEN `2f4da4f60721ee1799e319297f38ff92ab6e0c34`.
- **Repository:** `createCloudOrderRepository(db, { recoveryReader })` derives the mutation command once (`toMutationCommand`) for `mutateTransaction`, the new `confirmPendingIntent` and the new `cleanupConfirmedIntent` (both use `input.leaseEpoch` and the registered Lite reader); the identity APIs used by the Orders page are untouched.
- **Page:** one `MutationOperation` per mounted owner (attempt, attemptPromise, attemptSettled, querySequence, checking, retryAuthorized, receipt, timer). Ten seconds only prompt「結果確認中，勿重新收款」with the original command ID; the query is allowed while the original is in flight; retry needs a settled original attempt, a fresh successful Lite not-found that started after it settled, the same owner/dataset/epoch and being online (read failure, maintenance, quota, unsupported schema and different dataset never unlock it); a not-found query no longer resends by itself. A confirmed receipt (from the original result or from a query) runs `cleanupConfirmedIntent` once, then reloads the full account history; the correction controls and the navigation state (`recovering`) stay locked until the full re-read succeeds (contract §4.1); a re-read load error shows its message but does not release navigation, which stays `recovering` until a manual re-read succeeds; a late success or failure of the original never overrides the confirmed result. The registry identity now carries `datasetId`, `uid` and the frozen `input` (memory only, never the draft form); with no Auth uid the screen is cleared and nothing is sent. A definite refusal keeps the previous behavior (revision conflict and closed day reload, other codes show their message).
- **Existing unit assertions that changed (contract §4/§4.1, none relaxed):** (1) `recovers an unknown correction by retrying the frozen command id and revisions` (now `queries an unknown correction read-only and resends the frozen command only after an explicit retry click`): the same click used to query and, on not-found, resend automatically; it now queries without resending and only the explicit「以相同命令編號重試」click resends with the same command ID and frozen input, followed by one journal cleanup. (2) Recovery queries go through `confirmPendingIntent` (Lite) instead of `readCommand`, so mock results are `matched`/`not-found`/`different-dataset`. (3) Button text「查詢原命令並以同 ID 復原」to「查詢原命令結果」; the read-error message to the shared `目前無法安全確認原命令；…`; the identity not-found text stays. (4) The `RecoveryIdentity` confirmation cases additionally assert one journal cleanup with the identity's dataset and uid. The harness also mocks `firebase/auth` and `cloudRecoveryReader`, as the other cloud pages' tests do.

Every other file calls those repositories only through these pages. `grep -rn "getCloudRecoveryReader" frontend/src/components` (excluding tests) hits all seven command-sending files, and each has the ten-second prompt (`10_000` or `PROMPT_AFTER_MS`). `grep -rn "commitCommand\|mutateTransaction\|recordExport\|recordPrint" frontend/src/components/cloud frontend/src/components/screens/BackupScreen.tsx` (excluding tests) only finds the three direct calls in `CloudReportPage`, `CloudAccountHistoryPage` and `CloudOrdersPage`; the other pages send through their own intent-based repositories (`commitStudentCommand`, `commitDayVendorIntent`, `commitDayCloseIntent`, the backup command repository), each reaching `commitCommand` inside its repository (`grep -n "commitCommand" frontend/src/firebase/cloud*Repository.ts`).

| `CloudOrdersPage` | `CreateOrder`, `CreatePayment`, `CreateCashMovement`, `CreateRefund`, and the undo `VoidTransaction` | P13b2b1 (#533) | `confirms a real Emulator order while the committed write response is held past ten seconds` |

| `CloudDayVendorsPage` | `EnsureDay`, `SetOpeningCash`, `SetDayMenu`, `SaveVendor`, `RemoveVendor` | P13b2b2b (#535) | `confirms SetOpeningCash through the registered Lite reader...`; `keeps a confirmed DayMenu receipt locked when its fresh full read changes...`; `does not retry a DayVendor while its first commit is in flight...` |
| `CloudDayClosePage` | `EnsureDay`, `BackfillOpeningCash`, `StartBackfill`, `CloseDay`, `ReopenDay`, `RecordPostCloseEntry` | P13b2b2c (#536) | `confirms a real CloseDay through the registered Lite reader while the response is held past ten seconds`; `does not start the next backfill step while the first step response is held...` |

### P13c1 accessEvents Rules and lease-transaction writer 2026-10-23

- **Scope and lineage:** branch `claude/p13c1-access-events`, based on main `b879dd257efcb2353b108a4785d37f6940d37221` (#539 merged). Plan `claude/plan-p13cd` at `2e87b31af6e7f06170d85b7cffe00e7a379f0468` (not merged), batch P13c1 under planner rulings R1 (event id is the new epoch), R2 (atomicity bound by Rules in both directions), R3 (epoch never goes backward) and R4 (not in the P14 backup chain). RED `bae73802cc1df4730993724a621957eede59cc4f` (tests only); GREEN `6fad3f24a9d69067a9873dbd50277a8b2dfbcc6f`; docs commit is the PR head.
- **Rules:** `accessEvents/{epoch}` is create-only; `accessEventCreate` requires an approved operator, exactly the six fields, `after.epoch == before.epoch + 1`, `eventId == string(after.epoch)`, holder/previous holder equal to the lease after/before, `actorUid`/`actorEmail` equal to the caller, `at == request.time` and `kind` derived from the lease (`release` when the new holder is null, `acquire` when the old holder is null, otherwise `takeover`, including the same operator re-acquiring after their own expiry). The lease update gained one clause: when `epoch` changes, `accessEvents/{new epoch}` must not exist before the commit (`!exists`) and must exist after it (`existsAfter`); see the r1 rework below for why `!exists` is needed. Renew keeps the epoch, so it cannot carry an event and writes none. get/list need an approved operator; update/delete are denied.
- **Writer:** `acquireLease`/`releaseLease` take the actor email and `tx.set` the event in the same `runTransaction` as the lease update (`renewLease` untouched); `FirebaseProvider` passes the signed-in profile email. `firestorePaths.accessEventPath` and `CloudAccessEventDoc` added. The e2e helper `seedAccessLease` and `upsertDocument('system', 'accessLease', ...)` now delete events numbered above a reseeded epoch (Admin REST, demo Emulator only), otherwise a stale event would turn the next acquire into a denied update (R3 fail-closed behaviour; spec §4.2).
- **RED evidence:** in `activeAccountLease.spec.ts` (E01–E16) the writer cases and the hand-written positive control E07 failed (no events, so the batch was denied or the history empty); the negative guards E08–E16 pass by default deny before GREEN, which is expected. The five new unit cases in `activeAccountLease.unit.test.ts` failed for the missing `tx.set`.
- **Existing tests changed:** `activeAccountLease.spec.ts` and `.unit.test.ts` existing calls now pass the actor email argument (signature change only; no assertion weakened). After the RED commit the new `transition` helper's typing and one unused-variable lint error were fixed in the GREEN commit (no assertion change). `cloudRulesProof.spec.ts` is unchanged: it runs against its own committed fixture `fixtures/cloudRulesProof.rules`, not `firestore.rules`, and its raw lease-transition test still passes against that fixture.
- **Local verification (GREEN `6fad3f24a9d69067a9873dbd50277a8b2dfbcc6f` plus docs, Emulator fresh per command):** `activeAccountLease.spec.ts` 36/36 (CI command, no extra flags; 35/35 before the r1 rework added E17); `activeAccountLease.unit.test.ts` and provider tests pass; `firestoreRules.spec.ts` 10/10; `cloudRulesProof.spec.ts` 36/36; `cloudCommands`+`cloudCommandRepository.failure`+`cloudPendingJournal` 227/227; `cloudStudentRepository` 12/12; `cloudDayVendorRepository` 10/10; `cloudOrderRepository` 15/15; `cloudCashMovement` 42/42; `cloudRefund` 43/43; `cloudTransactionMutation` 47/47; `cloudDayClose`+`cloudOpeningCorrection` 240/240; `cloudReadRepository` 24/24; `cloudReportActions` 27/27; `npm run test:cloud-backup` 24/24 (R4: still run as a regression). The batch of 14 specs above ran with `--no-file-parallelism --hookTimeout=60000 --testTimeout=60000` (first-run Emulator warm-up exceeded the 10 s hook default once); the lease spec and backup were re-run with the plain CI commands. Full `npx vitest run --maxWorkers=1`: 195 files passed / 15 Emulator-only skipped, 2,225 passed / 770 skipped / 0 failed (2,220 / 753 before: +5 unit cases, +17 Emulator-only cases). Chromium (local build 1194 via an uncommitted executablePath override): default config 24/24, P12c 3/3; P13b2 29/29 on the first candidate and 28/29 after the rework, the single failure being `serializes real cross-tab journal writes...`, which also fails on unmodified main locally (CI passes); it passed in the earlier run. `npm run lint` clean; `npm run build` passes; `npx tsc -b` 211 diagnostics, the same as main. Not run: `test:cloud-reports` as its own script (the same `cloudReadRepository.spec.ts` ran in the batch above).
- **Rules measurement:** see [p05-rules-budget.md](p05-rules-budget.md) “P13c1”. Lease update `+94 / +95` on main versus `+93 / +94` on the branch (one added term); `accessEventCreate` `+97 / +98`; the `+hi` rows are compile boundaries (“Expression is too complex to evaluate safely”). Access calls: lease epoch change 2, event create 3.
- **Fold-ins:** (a) the P13b2b3c Page bullet above now says what `CloudAccountHistoryPage.tsx` does: after a confirmed receipt the controls and navigation stay locked until the full re-read succeeds, and a re-read load error shows its message but navigation stays `recovering` until a manual re-read succeeds (previously “or a load error shows”). (b) `60_000` timeouts added to `cloudReadRepository.spec.ts` `keeps short null-date queries bounded ...` (8.2 s locally, 101 sequential exports, loop unchanged) and `cloudCashMovement.spec.ts` `P09.5 ...` (1.1 s locally; it timed out on a slow main CI run). The other long loops in those two specs (`Array.from({ length: 10 })` in P09.5) are well under five seconds; no other timeout was added.
- **r1 rework (review REJECTED at head `e665f9ef1494394a65b48c927a321adb6df5d64b`, Blocking):** with only `existsAfter`, a stale `accessEvents/{epoch+1}` (for example left behind by a maintenance-created lease with too small an epoch) made the lease clause true although the commit wrote no event, so a raw-SDK approved operator could change the lease epoch without an event (reviewer probe: lease `null/0` plus event `1`, batch of a lease-only update to `{holderUid: A, epoch: 1}` was allowed); the spec's "fails closed" wording was therefore wrong for the raw path (the writer path was blocked only because `tx.set` on an existing event is a denied update). Fix: `!exists(accessEvents/{new epoch})` beside `existsAfter` (Gate 2 ruling option (a)). RED `b96088ad37aa38a7b0f59ee6b4b7161eb8e76195`: new Emulator case E17 (raw lease-only acquire and release with a stale event, plus an attempt to overwrite the stale event); against the old Rules it failed at the first lease-only assertion (checked by running it against the pre-fix Rules); an earlier version of the case also had a wrong final assertion, fixed before the RED commit was pushed. GREEN `1d095bcf755908b6e05119cc7a39bfc6fddba30f`: Rules only; `activeAccountLease.spec.ts` 36/36. The spec §4.2 wording now states the actual behaviour (a stale event blocks every acquire or release that would reach that epoch, through the writer or a raw SDK, and never lets an epoch change without a new event). Re-measured with the same cases: lease update `+93 / +94` before and after, `accessEventCreate` `+97 / +98` before and after; the epoch-changing lease update now makes 3 access calls (was 2), event create still 3.
- **Boundaries:** `accessEvents` is not in the P14 backup manifest/replay (no `seq`, not a command; spec §9.3). Rules and the frontend must be deployed together: an old frontend against the new Rules cannot acquire or release (its lease update has no event), and a new frontend against old Rules is denied on the event create. No deployment, production App Check or operator acceptance; P13c2 (access history UI), P13d paper backfill and overall P13 remain.

### P13c2 access handover table on the F2 report page 2026-10-23

- **Scope and lineage:** branch `claude/p13c2-access-audit`, based on main `ea31d76e8bb51874fe086f2b035da1e534b215f3` (#541 merged). Plan `claude/plan-p13cd` at `baf79a7ea5844b3744b818dc19921aca7218780d` (not merged), batch P13c2 under R5 (read-only, emails not UIDs, no CSV, no `RecordExport`). RED `4f66efd4c2f8c4c6033cd666a1481a30caaeead8` (tests only); fold-in (a) `9650a6a8eaa1f57e00400d91bc2579c0d61222a7`; fold-in (b) `211cfb0688d99c58950a72a05af7016c4d918ae6`; GREEN `6978c6324139850efdd0a111894395fbcbd0da83`; docs commit is the PR head.
- **Reader:** `readAccessEvents({ range, leaseEpoch })` in `cloudReadRepository.ts`. Query: `accessEvents` where `at >= Taipei midnight of startDate` and `at < Taipei midnight after endDate` (same `taipeiAuditTimestampRange` as the null-date audit query), `orderBy('at')`, `orderBy(documentId())`, page 100 through `readPaged`; limit 20,000 events (`maxAccessEvents` is a test seam) and anything above fails with `INCOMPLETE_SNAPSHOT`. Same lease rules as the other reads: `validateSnapshotScope` before (wrong epoch or another holder is `LEASE_LOST`) and `assertContextUnchanged` after every page and at the end (`STALE_SNAPSHOT` on a head or lease change). Each event is decoded with an exact six-field check, the kind must match its holder/previous-holder shape, the id must be a positive decimal epoch no larger than the current lease epoch; otherwise `UNSUPPORTED_SCHEMA`. Rows are sorted by numeric epoch (document ids sort as strings: `10` before `9`).
- **Previous holder email:** the event stores only `previousHolderUid`. The email comes from the event of the previous epoch (read from the loaded rows, or one `getDocFromServer` of `accessEvents/{epoch-1}` for the first row), accepted only when that event's `holderUid` and `actorUid` both equal `previousHolderUid`; otherwise the row says `unrecorded` and the page shows 「（無紀錄）」. `previousHolderUid == null` is `none` → 「無」. Events from before P13c1 was deployed do not exist, so the first epochs of a real project will show 「（無紀錄）」 for a previous holder; that is the honest answer, not a bug. UIDs are not part of the returned rows.
- **Page:** `CloudReportPage` reads the events in parallel with the report and audit when the scope is all accounts (a single-account report does not read them: handovers are not account facts; this is my reading of the plan, which does not name the scope). The table (time in Taipei, 取得／接手／釋放使用權, operator email, previous holder) is a section below the export/print section; it appears only after the report and audit already agree, and only if the events' dataset, H, go-live date, lease day, lease epoch and range equal the report's. A read failure (not `LEASE_LOST`/`UNAUTHORIZED`, which invalidate the page like any other read) or an envelope mismatch hides the table and says why; the report and its exports stay. No export button, no CSV, no `RecordExport`.
- **Index:** `firestore.indexes.json` unchanged. The query is one range on `at` ordered by `at` then `__name__`, which Firestore serves from the automatic single-field index. The Emulator does not enforce composite-index requirements, so the Emulator and E2E passes below do not prove this; it rests on the Firestore indexing rules and still needs the first real-project read to confirm there is no "requires an index" error (CheerC's local acceptance, not done here).
- **RED evidence:** `cloudReadRepository.spec.ts` (9 cases): `TypeError: readAccessEvents is not a function`, run with the Emulator; `CloudReportPage.test.tsx`: 5 of the 6 new cases failed (no table); the sixth (`does not show the table when the report and audit snapshots disagree`) is a negative guard that passes before GREEN, as expected. The E2E A11 below was added with GREEN and was not run against the tree without the table.
- **Existing tests changed:** none of the existing assertions. My own new Emulator cases were adjusted after the RED commit because GREEN added the "event number ≤ current lease epoch" check: the describe block now seeds the lease at epoch 30 and passes that epoch instead of 7, and a case for an event numbered after the lease was added (10 cases in total). The page test's `vi.mock` now also provides `readAccessEvents`, and `beforeEach` gives it a default implementation. `cloud-production.spec.ts` gained A11 (a new test, nothing else touched).
- **Local verification (GREEN plus docs, Emulator fresh per command):** `npm run test:cloud-reports` 34/34 (24 before + 10 new); `npx vitest run src/components/cloud/CloudReportPage.test.tsx` 61/61 (55 before + 6 new); `CloudDayClosePage.test.tsx` 76/76, three repeats plus two under 8 busy-loop processes on 4 CPUs; full `npx vitest run --maxWorkers=1` 190 files passed / 15 Emulator-only skipped, 2,206 passed / 780 skipped / 0 failed (2,200 / 770 before: +6 page cases, +10 Emulator-only cases); Chromium (local build 1194 via an uncommitted executablePath override): default config 26/26 (25 + A11), P12c 3/3; `npm run lint` clean; `npm run build` passes; `npx tsc -b` 210 diagnostics, the same as main. Not run: prototype and P13b2 Chromium configs (no file they exercise changed; the P13b2 cross-tab case fails on main locally anyway), `npm run test:cloud-backup` (no command kind, backup manifest or replay change; `accessEvents` is outside the P14 chain).
- **Fold-ins:** (a) the P13d3 review nit: `BACKEND-HANDOFF.md` (the `firebase/syncStatus.ts` row) and `pc-pos-order-flow-spec.md`, `order-ledger-cash-close-spec.md`, `student-account-management-spec.md` now say the old modules were deleted in P13d3 and point at the cloud path; archived, plan and review documents stay as written. (b) `CloudDayClosePage.test.tsx`: the multi-step backfill assertions (`已標為回填…`, `補開帳未完成…停在這一步`, `backfill-continue`, `backfill-plan-panel`) wait up to 5 s instead of the 1 s default, and the seven tests that contain them have an explicit 15 s test timeout; no assertion text, count or order changed. Root cause is the same class as the Emulator timeouts: several chained async steps after one click exceed 1 s on a loaded CI runner. Not reproduced locally even with the CPU saturated, so this is a margin, not a proven fix; other tests in that file were not widened.
- **Boundaries:** no Rules, indexes, command kind or backup change, so no Rules measurement and no P14 extension. No deployment, production App Check or operator acceptance. P13d1/d2 (paper backfill) and overall P13 remain.

### P13d1 paper number on every backfilled entry (note prefix) 2026-10-23

- **Scope and lineage:** branch `claude/p13d1-paper-ref`, based on main `29c537072e2a811b0f8fb3483b59d045103a4678` (#542 merged). Plan `claude/plan-p13cd` at `baf79a7ea5844b3744b818dc19921aca7218780d` (not merged), batch P13d1. Fold-in `87f5ad33821331c93eefeda15a81ad5f0f99a034`; RED `94044bc9aa3d8c0163af909ff246cda8593e705a` (tests only); GREEN `4e7ae13f3a9c364d479b95506e508113206a972f`; the docs commit is the PR head.
- **A versus B (automatic fallback):** CheerC chose A (an optional `paperRef` field). The plan said to fall back to B (a fixed note/reason prefix, no new field) if the thinnest anchor fell below +5 or dropped by more than 3. The A prototype dropped the backfilled `CreateOrder` transaction-create anchor from +16 to +9 (a drop of 7), so B shipped; the shipped B measures +14 (drop 2). All numbers are in [p05-rules-budget.md](p05-rules-budget.md) “P13d1”.
- **Rule:** on a marked, never-closed past day, `CreateOrder`/`CreatePayment` `note` and `CreateCashMovement` `reason` (stored as the transaction `note`) must match `紙本<1-20 of A-Za-z0-9->：…` (full-width colon, the text after it may be empty or multi-line, total still ≤500). Today needs nothing and may carry one. `CorrectTransaction` of a row whose `note` already has a paper number must keep that same number; the text after it can change; void does not touch `note`. `frontend/src/domain/paperRef.ts` has the format/parse helpers (`isPaperRef`, `formatPaperRefNote`, `parsePaperRefNote`) for P13d2.
- **RED evidence:** `paperRef.test.ts` could not import the module; in `cloudDayClose.spec.ts` against the old Rules `P13d1.1` (order, payment, cash movement: a missing or malformed number was accepted), `P13d1.4` and `P13d1.5` (a correction could drop or replace the number) failed with an `ok: true` result where `ok: false` was expected; `P13d1.2` and `P13d1.3` (valid number stored, today free) passed before GREEN as expected.
- **Existing tests changed (inputs, not assertions):** the `cloudDayClose.spec.ts` backfill helpers `backfillOrder`/`backfillPayment`/`backfillCash` now default to prefixed notes (`紙本A-0001：`, `紙本A-0002：`, `紙本A-0003：零錢補充`) because the new Rules refuse the old empty note on a backfill day; the two backfill cases of `cloudBackup.spec.ts` do the same; E2E D09 and D10 fill the existing 「備註（選填）」 field with a prefixed note (D09 also asserts the stored note); the P12c E2E (`cloud-report-history.spec.ts`) seeds its past-day orders and payments through the repository, so its helpers and the P13b2b3c payment now add the prefix `紙本P12C：` (the first CI run failed those two cases with `INVALID_INPUT`, which was the new Rules working as designed; local P12c config 3/3 after the change). No assertion was weakened or removed.
- **Backup (P14):** unchanged format. A backup that holds past-day entries without the prefix (everything before this rule) still validates and replays: `cloudBackup.spec.ts` strips the prefix from a real export and checks `validateBackupManifest` plus `replayBackupProjections`. Replay cannot require the prefix, because the manifest carries no version that tells old entries from new ones; the Rules are the only enforcement point.
- **Fold-in (#542 nit):** `readAccessEvents` now runs `assertContextUnchanged` before the `epoch > leaseEpoch` check, so a lease that moved on is a stale read instead of malformed data. No separate test: the stale-lease path is the same `assertContextUnchanged` the per-page check already tests.
- **Local verification (Emulator fresh per command):** `cloudDayClose.spec.ts` 205/205; every other Emulator spec in `src/firebase/__tests__` together (`--no-file-parallelism`, all `EASYORDER_REQUIRE_*` flags) 33 files / 796 passed / 0 skipped; `npm run test:cloud-backup` 24/24; `paperRef.test.ts` 5/5; full `npx vitest run --maxWorkers=1` 191 files passed / 15 Emulator-only skipped, 2,211 passed / 787 skipped / 0 failed; `npm run lint` clean; `npm run build` passes; `npx tsc -b` 210 diagnostics (same as main); Chromium (local binary) D09 and D10 pass against the Emulator.
- **Boundaries and deployment:** Rules and the frontend must go out together with P13d2. Until then the existing UI has no paper-number field, so an operator must type the prefix into the note field to record a backfilled entry; without it Rules refuse the write (shown as an input error). No production App Check, deployment or operator acceptance. P13d2 (UI fields, report/CSV/F5 display) and overall P13 remain.

### P13d2 paper number fields, locked correction, report/CSV/F5 display 2026-10-23

- **Scope and lineage:** branch `claude/p13d2-paper-ui`, based on main `f8ff80093863a53ce67026d07cabc7cfcc6b4f90` (#543 merged). Plan `claude/plan-p13cd` at `baf79a7ea5844b3744b818dc19921aca7218780d` (not merged), batch P13d2, adapted to the merged option B (the number is the note/reason prefix). RED `e677c2295d056dca377f16067a53b111449b2ee0` (tests only); GREEN code `ae5cd890d736c7b3230e2c09e47af66dd4c12cad`; E2E and spec `144652c4ae45675919bd5f1003815d88cf9a6bb6`; the docs commit is the PR head. No Rules, indexes, command kind or backup change.
- **What it does:** the Orders page order, payment and counter-cash forms have a separate 「紙本號」 field (required on a backfill day, optional today). `composePaperRefNote` builds the `紙本<n>：` prefix before submit and refuses a missing number (補登日必須填紙本號…), a malformed number (紙本號只能是 1 至 20 字的英數與 -…) or a note over 500 characters, each with its own message, before the duplicate-order confirmation and before any command. A correction (Orders edit modal, F5 correction form) shows the stored number as locked text and edits only the text after it; the sent note keeps the prefix. F2 report detail rows and the F5 transaction details show the number apart from the remark; the transaction CSV gets `paper_refs` as the **last** column (merged rows list the distinct numbers of their constituents, `;` separated), so the existing column order is unchanged.
- **Spec fold-in (#543 nit):** §7.4 now says that a prefix added to a today row locks that number on later corrections, and describes the P13d2 UI.
- **RED evidence:** before GREEN the new `paperRef.test.ts` helpers (`composePaperRefNote`, `splitPaperNote`, `paperRefsIn`) and the new page, report, F5 and CSV assertions failed (missing exports / no field / no `paper_refs` column).
- **Existing tests changed:** `CloudOrdersPage.test.tsx` six backfill submit tests now fill 「紙本號」 and assert the prefixed note (the backfill day cannot be submitted without it, per the plan); `cloudLedgerExport.test.ts` column-list assertion gains the last `'paper_refs'`; an empty-reason correction assertion reads the edit preview bar (the message shows there and in the alert); E2E D09/D10 use the new field instead of typing the prefix in the note field; E2E R01 (P12c) asserts the CSV last column and a report paper tag. No assertion was weakened or skipped.
- **Local verification (fresh run, branch head before docs):** full `npx vitest run --maxWorkers=1` 191 files passed / 15 Emulator-only skipped, 2,232 passed / 787 skipped / 0 failed; `npm run lint` clean; `npm run build` passes; `npx tsc -b` 210 diagnostics (same as main). Chromium (local binary) against the Emulator: default config 26/26 (includes D09 and D10 with the new field); P12c config 3/3 (R01 asserts the CSV last column and the report paper tag); P13b2 config 28 passed / 1 failed — the failure is the known local-Chromium cross-tab journal case `serializes real cross-tab journal writes…` (`p13b2-pending-journal.spec.ts:550`), which also fails on main in this environment and passes in CI, unrelated to this change (not skipped, not edited).
- **Local acceptance steps for CheerC (not done by the agent):** see the PR body (`npm run dev` against the Emulator: mark a past day as backfill, try to submit without a number, with a malformed one, then with `A-0123`; check the tag in the cash/details list, the F2 report, the CSV last column and the F5 details; correct the row and see the number locked).
- **Boundaries:** no production App Check, deployment or operator acceptance. Rules (P13d1) and this frontend must go out together. P13b2/P13c/P13d code is complete after this PR but nothing is deployed.

- **Scope and lineage:** branch `claude/p15a-restore-validate`, based on main `63043d68fe031e8e34978ed820ff324a062d88e7` (#545 merged). RED `99774b5557a1fa2613394ed02f2932cb495f37e9` (tests, helpers and a stub that exits 70, so the tests fail on behavior); GREEN `0d3a8602e2061ac7df7cc3c3a8092448e33bcf2a`; the docs commit is the PR head. No Rules, indexes, command kind or backup-format change.
- **What exists:** `scripts/easyorder-data-maintenance.mjs` (launcher: Node ≥ 22.18 check, `frontend/node_modules` check, `module.register` of `scripts/lib/ts-extension-hook.mjs`, then `frontend/src/maintenance/cli.ts` with the real terminal/file IO of `nodeIo.ts`). The hook only adds `.ts` (or `/index.ts`) to extensionless relative imports made from inside `frontend/src/`. The CLI imports the same `cloudBackup.ts` validation the app uses, so the CLI and the F3 backup screen cannot drift. `validate` reads the local chain file, hash-checks and decrypts each package, links the packages (R5), replays the first H commands from an empty state (default H = last `toSeq`), and at a package boundary also compares the package's own `projectionState` and `totals`. Subcommands `init`, `restore`, `resume`, `verify`, `finalize` print “尚未提供” and exit 64.
- **Chain file (R4):** `{ "format": "easyorder-restore-chain", "version": 1, "datasetId": "...", "packages": [{ "file": "...", "sha256": "<optional 64 hex>" }] }`; `file` resolves against the chain file's directory.
- **Exit codes:** 0 done; 64 usage error (unknown subcommand/flag, missing `--manifest`, malformed `--to-seq`, `--password` on the command line, subcommand not yet provided); 2 a check failed (wrong password or damaged package, chain file or hash mismatch, dataset or schema mix, a package that does not continue the previous one, first package not from seq 0, `--to-seq` beyond the last package); 1 unexpected; 69 environment (Node too old or `frontend/node_modules` missing). Decision for plan case A9: `--to-seq` beyond the last package exits **2** (it is only known after the chain is read); a non-integer value exits 64.
- **Password (R3):** read from stdin only (hidden on a TTY, first line of a pipe); `--password` and `--password=` are refused with exit 64 before any package is read.
- **Report (R12):** counts per collection, per-day `totals` and settlement-version counts, student count and balance sum, a SHA-256 digest per collection, `decryptMs`/`replayMs`/`totalMs`, and the planned write count (commands + projection documents + student-code index entries + the final dataHead) with estimated days at 18,000 writes a day. No name, student code, individual balance, UID, e-mail, remark, command ID or password; the Emulator test checks every source string.
- **R02 fixture:** generated per run through the production writers (`createCloudCommandRepository`, `createCloudOrderRepository`, `createCloudDayCloseRepository`, `createCloudBackupCommandRepository`) against `demo-easyorder`: students (one code change), a vendor, a back-filled gap day with paper-numbered entries and its close, today's menu / opening cash / order / payment / cash movement / refund / correction / void / cancel / undo / opening correction, `RecordBackup` receipts, close → reopen → re-close, and two post-close entries. It is exported as three encrypted increments (H = 29, 35, 37), plus one copy of the last package chained to a wrong previous hash and a one-command package of another dataset. No encrypted file is committed.
- **Tests:** RED failed on behavior (every case got exit 70 from the stub instead of 0/2/64). After GREEN: `cloudRestore.spec.ts` 13/13 (A1 full chain equals the source head, day totals, student count and balance sum; A2 H = 0, 12, 20 and the three package boundaries equal an independent replay; A3 wrong password; A4 flipped byte; A5 missing middle package; A6 wrong previous hash; A7 mixed dataset; A8 chain SHA-256 mismatch; A9 H beyond the end; A10 first package not from seq 0; A11 anonymity; A12 `--password`; help and “not provided yet”), `src/maintenance` unit tests 10/10 (usage, exit codes, empty-package chain failures).
- **Test changes after RED (disclosed):** A2 got a 60 s timeout (six child-process runs); A11 removes 64-hex digests before looking for source strings, because a SHA-256 can contain a four-digit student code by chance (a flaky check otherwise). The unit test file runs in the default jsdom environment, not `@vitest-environment node`: the shared `src/test/setup.ts` touches `window`, and the unit tests need nothing Node-specific (the real Node path is run by the Emulator spec).
- **Local verification (fresh run on the GREEN head):** `npm run test:cloud-restore` 13/13; `npm run test:cloud-backup` 24/24; full `npx vitest run --maxWorkers=1` 192 files passed / 16 Emulator-only skipped, 2,243 passed / 800 skipped / 0 failed; `npm run lint` clean; `npm run build` passes; `npx tsc -p tsconfig.maintenance.json --noEmit` 0 diagnostics; `npx tsc -b` 209 diagnostics (main 210: `domain/ledger.ts` lacked a type-only import that the new maintenance config would otherwise have surfaced, and the fix also drops one app diagnostic); `node scripts/easyorder-data-maintenance.mjs --help` prints the usage with Node 22.22 (CI uses Node 24).
- **Boundaries:** no production App Check, deployment or operator acceptance; nothing was run against a non-`demo-` project and no credentials are involved. `init`/`restore`/`resume` (P15b), `verify`/`finalize` (P15c), the runbook and Emulator drill evidence (P15d), and the CheerC-local empty Spark project drill remain; the real-Spark restore is UNVERIFIED.

### P15b init, restore and resume into an isolated project 2026-10-23

- **Scope and lineage:** branch `claude/p15b-restore-execute`, based on main `c96c0325bd9ebfc59d37227e30e09143e465a920` (#546 merged). Plan `claude/project-thread-0ee9ti` at `2f7faecba754b15e7fa28566600eb1d20dcfa2f1` (not merged), batch P15b. RED `38b89a713317664ae3927240b1a67267a747cc5d` (tests plus a REST stub that throws; the CLI still answered “尚未提供”, so the tests fail on behavior); GREEN `39917f71118120e8abe21d4832257938cccbe16f`; the docs commit is the PR head. No Rules, indexes, command kind or backup-format change (“Rules unchanged, nothing to measure”).
- **Target (R2):** with `FIRESTORE_EMULATOR_HOST` the CLI talks to that Emulator as `owner` and only for a `demo-` project; without it, only to `https://firestore.googleapis.com` with `EASYORDER_ACCESS_TOKEN` and never for a `demo-` project. A wrong pairing exits 64 before the chain file is read, before the password is asked and with zero fetch calls (unit test with an injected `fetch`). The token only goes into the `Authorization` header; the tests check it appears in no output or body. A real project's `--execute` also needs a terminal and the typed phrase `RESTORE <project> <datasetId> <H>` (`INIT <project> <datasetId>` for `init`); a dry run needs neither.
- **Plan (R6):** documents in a fixed order — `commands/{id}` by seq (the backup record minus `datasetId` and `schemaVersion`), projection documents by path, `studentCodes/{code}` derived from the command history (the last Create/ChangeStudentCode that set the code, changed-away codes removed, `updatedAt` = that command's `committedAt`) and cross-checked against the replayed student documents (a mismatch fails the plan), then the dataHead update (`seq`, `lastCommandId`, `updatedAt` only, as the Rules allow) alone in the last batch. At most 100 documents and 8 MiB a batch (`--batch-size` 1–100 lowers the first limit; it is part of the plan digest, so a resume must use the same value). Integers are `integerValue`; a non-safe integer, a float or a timestamp finer than a microsecond fails closed. The digest is the SHA-256 of the dataset, H and every batch.
- **Preconditions (R8, read-only):** dataHead exists, `maintenance == true`, `seq == 0`, `lastCommandId == null`, same `datasetId`, `goLiveDate` and `schemaVersion` as the chain's first command, and `commands`, `days`, `students`, `studentCodes`, `transactions`, `vendors`, `backfillDays`, `postCloseEntries` and the `settlements` collection group each list empty (`pageSize=1`). `init` creates the maintenance dataHead only when no dataHead and none of those documents exist, and prints a manifest that matches `docs/ops/p05-datahead-bootstrap-manifest.schema.json` (its `bootstrapMethod` is a schema constant; the tool actually writes through REST).
- **Resume (R7, R10):** every write carries `exists=false`, so no document is ever written twice. The progress file (`<chain dir>/.easyorder-restore-<project>-<datasetId>-<H>.json`, atomic write, only plan digest, batch counts and write count) is created before the first commit. `--max-writes` (default 18,000) stops at the batch boundary with exit 75 and the remaining write count; a limit smaller than one batch is a usage error (64) before anything is written. `resume` needs the progress file, the same digest and the same project/dataset/H, re-checks that the last recorded batch really exists on the target, then looks at the target batch by batch until the first batch that is not there: a batch that is fully there and equal is adopted without rewriting (a crash between commit and progress), a batch fully absent is committed, anything partial or different fails closed (exit 2). Emulator-only seam `EASYORDER_EMULATOR_CRASH_AFTER_BATCH=k` throws after batch k commits and before its progress is written.
- **Exit codes:** as P15a plus 75 (paused at the write limit); a target access failure (network, HTTP error) is 1 with a status-only message that never echoes the response body.
- **Tests:** RED failed on behavior. After GREEN: `cloudRestore.spec.ts` 31/31 — the 13 P15a cases plus B1 dry run writes nothing (explicit and by default) and leaves no progress file; B2 full restore equals the source document by document (all business collections, `studentCodes`, `settlements`; dataHead equal except `maintenance: true`); B2b restore to H = 12; B2c outputs and progress file anonymous; B3 re-export of the target equals the source `records`, `projectionState` and `totals`; B4 non-empty target, B5 not in maintenance, B6 never initialised (execute and dry run), B7 other dataset — all exit 2 with nothing written; B8 wrong target exits 64; B9 `--max-writes` pauses with exit 75 and `resume` finishes with the two runs' writes adding up to the plan size and the progress file agreeing; B9b a limit below one batch exits 64; B10 crash after batch 0, 1 and the last batch resumes to an identical target; B11 another H, batch size or chain exits 2 with the target unchanged; B12 a forged `completedBatches` and a junk progress file exit 2; B13 after a restore the production writer is refused until `maintenance` is cleared (then the same command is accepted, so maintenance was the only blocker); B14 `init` refuses an existing dataHead or a stray business document; init dry run versus `--execute` plus manifest shape. Unit: `firestoreRest.test.ts` 14/14 (B15: integers of every size and sign, nested map/array/null/empty, microsecond timestamps, fail-closed values, decode round trip, target resolution, request bodies, 409 mapping, batchGet, one-row list and collection-group queries) and `cli.test.ts` 17/17 (B8 with zero fetch calls and no password read, flag validation, typed-confirmation handling against a fake real-project target). Some B8 cases already exit 64 in RED because the stub subcommands also exit 64; they are not behavior proof until GREEN and are paired with the zero-fetch and no-password-read assertions.
- **Test changes after P15a (disclosed):** the A11 anonymity check now removes SHA-256 digests and the fixture's student codes are six digits (`731901`–`731903`), so no count or timing can contain one (reviewer nit from #546); the fixture generation order is now other-dataset package first, R02 source last, so the R02 source stays in the Emulator for the restore comparisons; `r02Scenario.ts` exports `R02_LEASE_EPOCH` and `operatorUser`; the unit harness gained `env`, `fetch`, `isTty`, `readLine` and `clock`; the P15a “later subcommands” check now uses `verify`/`finalize`. B13 uses `CreateStudent` instead of the plan's `CreatePayment`: the fixture's days are all closed, and any production-writer command shows the same maintenance block.
- **Local verification (Node 22.22):** `npm run test:cloud-restore` 31/31; `npm run test:cloud-backup` 24/24; `npx vitest run src/maintenance` 31/31; full `npx vitest run --maxWorkers=1` 193 files passed / 16 Emulator-only skipped, 2,264 passed / 818 skipped / 0 failed; `npm run lint` clean; `npm run build` passes; `npx tsc -p tsconfig.maintenance.json --noEmit` 0 diagnostics; `npx tsc -b` 209 diagnostics (same as main); `node scripts/easyorder-data-maintenance.mjs --help` prints the new usage. CI runs the Emulator spec in the existing P15 restore proof step (renamed; total ≥ 1, failed = 0, skipped = 0).
- **Boundaries:** only `demo-` Emulator projects and synthetic data; nothing was run against a real project and no credential is involved, so the real-Spark restore (token path, TTY confirmation, daily quota across days) is UNVERIFIED and stays CheerC's local drill after P15c. `verify`/`finalize` (P15c: full read-back comparison, operators, empty lease with a safe epoch, clearing maintenance) are not provided; a restored target stays in maintenance and has no operators or lease. No production App Check, deployment or operator acceptance.
- **docs-check:** `docs-check: arch=N adr=N area=Y` — this checkpoint, `PROJECT-STATUS.md`, `DEVELOPMENT-HANDOFF.md`; `firebase-backup-runbook.md` and spec §9.3 stay as they are until P15d.

### P15c verify and finalize 2026-10-24

- **Scope and lineage:** branch `claude/p15c-verify-finalize`, based on main `582cede85a414c8f41acfb8a73c2d39c815d10b0` (#547 merged). Plan `claude/project-thread-0ee9ti` at `2f7faecba754b15e7fa28566600eb1d20dcfa2f1` (not merged), batch P15c. RED `cad7b4ff269f84ee17a1fea433f66e89b9904e9b` (tests plus REST read-method stubs that throw; `verify`/`finalize` still answered “尚未提供”, so the Emulator tests fail on behavior); GREEN `f4e43ad4a1e3501c161ca64c6a1fac9004911b4b`; the docs commit is the PR head. No Rules, indexes, command kind or backup-format change (“Rules unchanged, nothing to measure”).
- **verify:** reads every business document of the target (`commands`, `days`, `students`, `studentCodes`, `transactions`, `vendors`, `backfillDays`, `postCloseEntries` through paged REST lists, and each day's `settlements`), and compares each with the plan the chain implies: per category expected / actual / missing / extra / different. It also recomputes from the target alone: per-day totals (`recalculateBackupTotals`, the same function as the backup replay), each student's balance from opening balance and the target's transactions (`deriveStudentBalance`, the production projection math) against `currentBalance`, command `seq` 1..H with no gap, settlement versions 1..n per day, and `dataHead` (`datasetId`, `seq`, `lastCommandId`). The report holds counts only (R12): no path, name, code, balance, UID or e-mail; it is written even when the result is a failure, and a failure exits 2. `maintenance` is reported, not judged, so verify also runs after finalize. It is read-only; the report lists the number of documents read (about one read per document).
- **finalize (R11):** without `--execute` it is a dry run (verify, then the planned operator and lease writes, nothing written). It refuses (exit 2, nothing written) when the progress file is missing or not at the last batch, when `dataHead.maintenance` is already false (“finalize does not run twice”), or when verify fails. Then, in one batch: operators from `--operators <local.json>` (`[{uid,email}]`, at least one, no other keys, no duplicate uid; built by `operatorDoc.ts`, kept equal to the app's `buildOperatorDoc` by `operatorDocParity.test.ts`) and an empty lease `{ holderUid: null, epoch: E, updatedAt }` with `E = max(existing lease epoch, highest accessEvents document number, highest command leaseEpoch)` (spec §4.2 lower bound); and only afterwards `dataHead.maintenance = false` with a `currentDocument.updateTime` precondition read just before. Re-running after a crash between the two writes reuses an operator or lease that already matches, and refuses one that does not (a held lease, a lease epoch below an `accessEvents` number, an operator with other content). A real project's `--execute` needs a terminal and the typed phrase `FINALIZE <project> <datasetId> <H>`. Output names counts only, never an operator's UID or e-mail. `accessEvents` are not restored (spec §9.2).
- **Tests:** RED failed on behavior. After GREEN `cloudRestore.spec.ts` 41/41 — the 31 earlier cases plus: C1 verify passes and its counts equal the offline validate's for all nine categories (and the output is anonymous); C2 an extra transaction (extra 1, exit 2, report written); C3 a changed student balance (different 1, balance mismatch 1); C4 a deleted command (missing 1, one seq gap); C5 finalize refuses an unfinished restore and, after completion, a target that fails verify, with no operator, no lease and maintenance still on; C6 dry run writes nothing, then finalize creates the operator, lease at epoch 7 (the fixture's highest leaseEpoch) and clears maintenance, and verify still passes; C7 an existing `accessEvents/12` lifts the lease epoch to 12 and the production `acquireLease` then acquires epoch 13 with its event; C8/C9 after finalize the operator acquires the lease, `ReopenDay` and `CreatePayment` through the production writers succeed (seq H+2, balance up by the payment), and `exportRange` on the target continues the source's last package (`previousPackageSha256` equal, records H+1 and H+2); C10 finalize again exits 2 and changes nothing; C11 finalize after a crash between its writes completes, and a held lease or a lease below an `accessEvents` number is refused. Unit: `firestoreRest.test.ts` 19/19 (adds paged list, ID-only list, update time, `updateTime` precondition and failure) and `cli.test.ts` 19/19 (adds verify/finalize flags, wrong target with zero fetch and no password read, operators-file errors before any network call); `operatorDocParity.test.ts` 2/2.
- **Test changes (disclosed):** the P15a/P15b “later subcommands are not provided” checks are replaced because every subcommand now exists (help lists all and no longer says “尚未提供”; an unknown subcommand still exits 64); several P15c unit cases (wrong target, missing flags) already exit 64 in RED because the subcommands did, so they are not behavior proof until GREEN; C8 reopens today's day before the payment (every fixture day is closed), so the payment is seq H+2, not H+1 as the plan table says; C7 uses `accessEvents/12` because the fixture's commands all carry leaseEpoch 7, not 3.
- **Local verification (Node 22.22):** `npm run test:cloud-restore` 41/41; `npm run test:cloud-backup` 24/24; `npx vitest run src/maintenance` 38/38; full `npx vitest run --maxWorkers=1` 194 files passed / 16 Emulator-only skipped, 2,273 passed / 828 skipped / 0 failed; `npm run lint` clean; `npm run build` passes; `npx tsc -p tsconfig.maintenance.json --noEmit` 0 diagnostics; `npx tsc -b` 209 diagnostics (same as main); `node scripts/easyorder-data-maintenance.mjs --help` lists verify and finalize. CI runs the Emulator spec in the existing P15 restore proof step.
- **Boundaries:** only `demo-` Emulator projects and synthetic data; nothing ran against a real project, so the real-Spark path (token, terminal confirmation, quota, `accessEvents` of a reused project) is UNVERIFIED and stays CheerC's local drill. Not in this PR: the runbook, the recorded Emulator drill with timings, spec §9.3 text and the plan status line (P15d). No production App Check, deployment or operator acceptance.
- **docs-check:** `docs-check: arch=N adr=N area=Y` — this checkpoint, `PROJECT-STATUS.md`, `DEVELOPMENT-HANDOFF.md`; `firebase-backup-runbook.md` and spec §9.3 stay as they are until P15d.

### P15d restore runbook and Emulator drill 2026-10-24

- **Scope and lineage:** branch `claude/p15d-runbook`, based on main `7ec6ad95b2658b51b930d8065552bb8e338b8fd9` (#548 P15c merged). Plan `claude/project-thread-0ee9ti` at `2f7faecba754b15e7fa28566600eb1d20dcfa2f1` (not merged), batch P15d. Docs plus one reviewer Nit from P15c, so the code part has RED `07742e4cc9e6b24398e0c68db1bd57bd2e1401f6` and GREEN `247c809bc21dab89f6a1be9b2b1ef738d3f45c48`; the docs commits and the final head are in the PR body (the version authority).
- **Code change (Nit from the P15c review):** `verify` listed `settlements` only under days that exist, so a settlements document whose `days/<d>` parent document does not exist (an orphan) was never read. It now lists `settlements` as a collection group (`runQuery`, `allDescendants`, ordered by name, paged by cursor, 300 per page) and counts any `*/*/settlements/*` that the plan does not contain as extra, so the report is `ok=false` and the command exits 2. RED: unit test of the group listing and `cloudRestore.spec.ts` C12 failed on behavior (verify exited 0 for an orphan). GREEN: C12 passes, `cloudRestore.spec.ts` 42/42. Also removed a stale “(P15c 提供)” from the `restore` completion message.
- **Drill fixture generator:** `npm run restore:drill-fixture` (needs `EASYORDER_P15_FIXTURE_DIR`) writes the synthetic R02 chain (`chain.json` plus three encrypted packages) so anyone can follow runbook section 3. It does nothing without that variable (one skipped test in a normal run). Password `p15-fixture-password` is a fixture constant, never a real secret.
- **Runbook:** new [firebase-restore-runbook.md](firebase-restore-runbook.md) (when and who approves, prerequisites and chain file, Emulator drill, the CheerC-only independent Spark drill, real-incident order from spec §9.3, write quota and splitting over days, RPO/RTO recording, privacy). `firebase-backup-runbook.md`'s “Restore ownership and limits” now links to it and no longer says the restore CLI is unavailable. Spec §9.3 gained a description of the CLI (subcommands, Emulator/REST target selection, `exists=false` batches, the epoch floor, the final `maintenance=false` write); no contract meaning changed.
- **Emulator drill, from a clean clone (Node 22.22, `npm ci`, then runbook section 3 verbatim at the PR's docs head):** synthetic chain 37 commands in 3 packages, 2 days, 4 settlement versions, 2 students, 12 transactions; `validate` exit 0, 64 documents to write, decrypt 393 ms (PBKDF2, three packages) and replay 8 ms; `init` dry run then `--execute` exit 0; `restore --dry-run` exit 0 (64 documents in 2 batches, target check passed); `restore --execute` exit 0, 64/64 in 2 batches, 814 ms of writes; `verify` exit 0, every category expected = actual, 64 documents read in 294 ms, command seq contiguous, day totals match, 2 balances rechecked 0 mismatches, settlement versions contiguous; `finalize` dry run exit 0 (1 operator to create, lease epoch 7); `finalize --execute` exit 0 (157 ms of verify reads); `verify` afterwards exit 0 with maintenance cleared; a second `finalize --execute` exit 2 (“已經解除維護”). Pause and resume on a second `demo-` project: `--max-writes 30 --batch-size 10` wrote 30 (3 of 8 batches) and exited 75, `resume` wrote the other 34 and exited 0, `verify` passed. Each command took about 1 s wall clock including Node start (the first command against a cold Emulator took about 5 s).
- **Not drill evidence:** these are Emulator numbers on 64 documents. They are not a real RTO, and say nothing about real Spark latency, quota, token handling, the terminal confirmation or `accessEvents` of a reused project.
- **Real Spark drill: UNVERIFIED, awaiting CheerC.** Runbook section 4 is the procedure; anonymous numbers go into this section in a later PR. P15 acceptance (“別人照 runbook 在乾淨 Emulator 與獨立 Spark 空庫恢復”) is therefore not complete.
- **Local verification (Node 22.22):** `npm run test:cloud-restore` 42/42; `npm run test:cloud-backup` 24/24; `npx vitest run src/maintenance` 39/39; full `npx vitest run --maxWorkers=1` 194 files passed / 17 skipped, 2,274 passed / 830 skipped / 0 failed (the skips are the Emulator-only suites and the opt-in fixture generator); `npm run lint` clean; `npm run build` passes; `tsc -p tsconfig.maintenance.json` 0 diagnostics; `tsc -b` 209 diagnostics (same as main). Rules unchanged, so no measurement; no new command kind, index or backup format.
